# Skills · E · part 39

814 entries, emotional-recap to employment-legal-hiring-review. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **emotional-recap**: Reviews the user's past Claude Code conversations from a wellbeing perspective — sentiment, tone, emotional arc, recurring patterns — and generates a supportive, science-grounded report in both Markdown and HTML. Default lookback is 48 hours across all projects. Uses recognised emotion frameworks (Plutchik, Ekman, Russell's circumplex, Pennebaker linguistic markers) and cites the science behind every observation. Learns the user's baseline tone over time so future reports flag genuine shifts, not noise. Use when the user asks for an emotional/wellbeing recap, mood check, sentiment review, or… ([mostafa-drz/claude-skills](https://github.com/mostafa-drz/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/code/emotional-recap) · ★5)
- **emotional-reel**: Create an emotional Thai "โดนใจ" vertical Reel (9:16 text-over-aesthetic-gradient frames) for a target audience — slow-reveal text with a pain → open-loop → realization → hope arc, rendered as ready-to-post PNG frames, plus caption + posting notes. Use for feels/drama short-form content (Reels/TikTok/Shorts) aimed at SME/entrepreneurs or any audience. Follows T.R.U.S.T. honesty: sell REAL emotion only, no fabricated numbers/claims, end on real hope, gentle brand tie (never salesy). - **empiriolabs-3d**: Generate 3D models with EmpirioLabs AI from a picture of an object or, on models that take text, from a description, wait for the job to finish, and download the file. Runs a bundled Python script that picks a model from the live catalog (or the one you name) and saves the file to the path you choose. - **empiriolabs-chat**: Ask an EmpirioLabs AI text model a question and get its answer. Use when the user asks for a second opinion from another model on code, a plan, or a draft, wants a specific EmpirioLabs AI model to answer, wants several models to answer the same prompt side by side, or needs a model that reads images to describe a picture. Runs a bundled Python script that picks a text model from the live catalog (or the ones you name), can attach text files and images to the prompt, and prints the answer or saves it to the path you choose. - **empiriolabs-image**: Generate and edit images with EmpirioLabs AI. Runs a bundled Python script that picks a model from the live catalog (or the one you name), waits for the result, and saves the files to the path you choose. - **empiriolabs-models**: Find the right EmpirioLabs AI model for a task and look up its price, parameters, limits, and supported endpoints in the live public catalog. - **empiriolabs-music**: Generate music and sound effects with EmpirioLabs AI from a text description, with optional lyrics on models that sing, wait for the result, and save the audio file. Runs a bundled Python script that picks a model from the live catalog (or the one you name) and saves the file to the path you choose. ([EmpirioLabs-AI/skills](https://github.com/EmpirioLabs-AI/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/empiriolabs-music))
- **empiriolabs-ocr**: Read PDFs and images with EmpirioLabs AI document OCR and return their text as Markdown, with tables, formulas, and reading order kept. Runs a bundled Python script that takes an https URL or a local file, picks a model from the live catalog (or the one you name), and prints the Markdown or saves it to the path you choose. ([EmpirioLabs-AI/skills](https://github.com/EmpirioLabs-AI/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/empiriolabs-ocr))
- **empiriolabs-search**: Search the web with EmpirioLabs AI and get current results, one answer with its sources, or a research report with citations. Runs a bundled Python script that picks a search model from the live catalog (or the one you name) and prints the results or saves them to the path you choose. - **empiriolabs-speech**: Turn text into natural speech with EmpirioLabs AI and save the audio file (MP3, WAV, and other formats). - **empiriolabs-transcribe**: Transcribe audio or video files to text with EmpirioLabs AI, from an https URL or a local file. Runs a bundled Python script that picks a transcription model from the live catalog (or the one you name) and waits for the result. - **empiriolabs-video**: Generate short videos with EmpirioLabs AI from a text prompt or a start image, wait for the job to finish, and download the MP4. Kullanıcı "şunu değiştir", "işyeri ekle", "profilimi güncelle", "ayarlarımı düzenle" ya da "customize" dediğinde kullan. "[Ülke]de işe alıyoruz", "[ülke]ye genişleme", "[ülke]de ilk çalışan" ya da "yabancı şirket Türkiye'de işe alacak" denildiğinde kullan. "Bu teklifi incele", "rekabet yasağı koyabilir miyiz", "iş sözleşmesine bak", "yurt dışından işe alım" denince ya da teklif eklendiğinde kullan.
